# Every-screen Tactile Spatial contract — canonical Rev 1.11

**228 context obligations** are retained, not that many top-level pages. All new native UX/rendering follows BG-001 and the completed BE-032 catalogue. Only the canonical plan schedules work; historical IDs remain traceable.

## VNX-S-HOME-01 — Spatial Home
**Source:** REV2_SURFACE; **presentation:** NAVIGATION_CONTEXT; **family:** ORB_AND_COMPACT_SUMMARY
**Backend:** BE-030; **frontend:** FE-004
**Requirements:** VAN-NX-004, VAN-NX-005, VAN-NX-007, VAN-NX-063

One orb and one priority statement; at most three concise hub groups. No state legend.

**Dark/light:** Both locked references; native goldens per state family after BG-001.
**Authority:** Navigation never executes. Effectful control opens its exact native review and owning service gate.
**States:** LOADING, READY, EMPTY, STALE, OFFLINE, ERROR, UNKNOWN, ACCESS_DENIED, OUTCOME_UNKNOWN
**Qualification:** T-SURFACE-001; physical S24 and native both-theme acceptance after backend qualification.

**Native feature controls:**
- `VNX-F-HOME-01` — Spatial Home. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.
- `VNX-F-HOME-02` — State-aware metallic orb. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.
- `VNX-F-HOME-04` — Attention preview. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.
- `VNX-F-HOME-05` — Smart briefing. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.
- `VNX-F-HOME-06` — Semantic deep links. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.
- `VNX-F-HOME-07` — Home state fallbacks. Expose native context-appropriate view/draft/exact-review affordance; do not leak every internal field or execute from navigation.

Keep primary intent dominant; detailed metrics/actions belong in contextual sheets/tabs. No generic flat reskin, rainbow controls, duplicate global navigation or Home state legend. Unsupported/stale/unknown sources remain honest and all meaningful gestures have accessible alternatives.

## van.browser.interactive — Remote Browser
**Source:** LEGACY78; **presentation:** native_activity; **family:** TASK_AND_SPATIAL_FLOW
**Backend:** BE-025; **frontend:** FE-017
**Requirements:** VAN-NX-014, VAN-NX-015, VAN-NX-016, VAN-NX-017, VAN-NX-069, VAN-NX-023

Focused subgraph/phase summary, meaningful agents/edges, no dense desktop board squeezed onto phone; accessible list.

**Dark/light:** Both themes and every applicable state; native render not yet accepted.
**Authority:** Preserve the exact inherited owner-action, scope, approval and independent readback chain; navigation never executes.
**States:** LOADING, READY, EMPTY, STALE, OFFLINE, ERROR, UNKNOWN, ACCESS_DENIED, OUTCOME_UNKNOWN
**Qualification:** T-SURFACE-142; physical S24 and native both-theme acceptance after backend qualification.

**Original outcome:** Browse under an owner device lease.

**All inherited action obligations:**
- inspect tabs/target/events
- owner touch and keyboard
- pause/resume/close
- take control
- delegate current control to an existing mission
- review downloads and explicit save/upload/copy/paste
- Confirm the current browser viewport
- Pause this browser session
- Request remote browser closure and distinguish local disconnect
- Read current target/tab inventory and safe session event history
- Resume the exact admitted session with fresh readback
- Take control only after matching fresh owner lease and generation
- Delegate the exact current lease to an existing mission and require authoritative readback
- Review actual owned-session download records
- Remove an eligible host download record and confirm exact DELETED readback
- Save verified browser download bytes through the Android Storage Access Framework
- Choose and upload a bounded phone document only for an explicit current browser chooser
- Explicitly copy browser selection or paste phone clipboard under current owner control
- Prepare an actual browser task, review exact click/fill effects and observe the sealed result without re-actuation
- Inspect native file analysis and exact import operation readbacks, with bound hash/epoch and provider limits
- Open independently verified selected bytes in VAN's bounded inert native viewer
- Read current archive/candidate provider capability, exact admitted destination and unavailable/refused states
- Prepare and independently recover the exact immutable file/destination request before any provider effect
- Approve the exact file request with fresh native A4 and inspect separately admitted provider claim and persisted-byte evidence
- Explicitly check an already claimed external byte receipt after an uncertain effect without resubmitting or changing the original outcome
